{"type":"message_update","usage":{"input":2942,"output":2837,"cacheRead":1024,"cacheWrite":0,"reasoning":0,"totalTokens":6803,"cost":{"input":0,"output":0,"cacheRead":0,"cacheWrite":0,"total":0}},"assistantMessageEvent":{"type":"thinking_end","contentIndex":0,"content":"\nWe need to fix render_rows function to support mark_last parameter. Currently render_rows is defined in render.py as:\n\ndef render_rows(rows):\n    return [\",\".join(str(v) for v in r) for r in rows]\n\nBut the test expects it to be called with mark_last=True and return [\"1,2\", \"TOTAL,3,4\"] for input [[1, 2], [3, 4]].\n\nActually the test: assert render_rows([[1, 2], [3, 4]], mark_last=True) == [\"1,2\", \"TOTAL,3,4\"]\n\nSo render_rows should accept a mark_last keyword argument and when True, mark the last row somehow. Looking at the export_csv function we just edited, it calls render_rows(rows) and then if totals, it creates a totals_row and does render_rows([totals_row])[0]. But the test_render_marks_last_row_as_total suggests there is a separate behavior for marking the last row as total in the render_rows function itself.\n\nHowever, the export_csv function now handles totals by appending a totals row after rendering all rows.
